# 全库逐组件深度对齐审计 — 2026-10-08

检查全部153个canonical U*组件；Ui*兼容别名共用实现，不重复计数。基准为官方Vuetify 4.2.4发布包的类型声明与运行源码；保留本库外观。130项初步上游映射、23项本库扩展或无直接映射，最终映射结论见各行。

此轮为用户要求的逐项检查，未批量修改产品行为、依赖、组件样式或用例。结果区分名称缺口、类型/default/event/slot约定、具体语义与真实用例；源码存在实现路径不等于浏览器实测，未确认缺口不代表完全兼容。

外观/本库约定差异也逐项列出，包含尚未实现的上游样式入口；这类属性不计入行为缺口，但不能当作已支持，也不表示用户已批准永久省略。模型、路由、布局尺寸、事件和插槽的契约差异仍须单独查看。Root复核与优先级见[ROOT-REVIEW.md](ROOT-REVIEW.md)。

验收层次：152项源码对照、0项采用已有专项证据、1项本轮专项运行。逐项原始结果见results-1/2/3.json，覆盖与行引用检查及当前源码SHA256见validation.json。

结论分布：存在差异 / 部分覆盖117项；未确认行为缺口10项；本库扩展23项；同名职责不同3项。属性候选共2454项，按组件计数，不代表唯一属性或严重程度；全部已分区记录。

## 逐组件证据

### UButton

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。本地默认 outlined/md/button/ripple=true；loading 会保留按钮尺寸，disabled/loading 阻止点击，组内状态由本库 ButtonGroup 提供。

上游：[VBtn 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VBtn/VBtn.tsx)。

缺失属性：`replace`、`exact`、`active`、`activeColor`、`readonly`、`appendIcon`、`border`、`elevation`、`hoverElevation`、`tag`、`height`、`maxHeight`、`maxWidth`、`minHeight`、`minWidth`、`width`、`location`、`position`、`baseColor`、`symbol`、`flat`、`block`、`slim`、`stacked`、`spaced`。

缺失事件：`group:selected`。

缺失插槽：`prepend`、`append`。

- 契约：本地默认 outlined/md/button/ripple=true；loading 会保留按钮尺寸，disabled/loading 阻止点击，组内状态由本库 ButtonGroup 提供。
- 契约：本地虽接受 to 对象，但只把字符串 to 赋给 href；官方 VBtn 使用 useLink/useRouter，消费者装有 RouterLink 时仍解析路由对象并提供导航/active 状态。
- 契约：本地支持 default/loader；官方还提供 prepend/append 内容槽及图标 props。
- 契约：VBtn 另发 group:selected；本地按钮组选择状态不向 UButton 消费者发同名事件。prependIcon/text/tile 分别由 icon prop、默认槽、rounded=false 表达；appendIcon 等仍列为缺失。
- 属性候选逐项判定：29项；确认缺失25、实现/转发0、保留约定4、待验证0。
- 外观/本库约定差异属性：`theme`、`tile`、`prependIcon`、`text`。

- 属性依据：missing 分组：border, elevation, hoverElevation, tag, height, maxHeight, maxWidth, minHeight, minWidth, width, location, position, replace, exact, active, activeColor, baseColor, symbol, flat, appendIcon, block, readonly, slim, stacked, spaced。这些候选未见本地组件行为实现，按属性协议缺口记录。
- 属性依据：intentionallyDifferent 分组：theme, tile, prependIcon, text。theme 保留 UI 自身 tokens；tile 由 rounded=false 表达；text 用默认内容槽；prependIcon 由本地 icon prop 在默认内容前渲染。appendIcon、路由、尺寸和交互候选仍按缺失列出。

- 属性依据：unverified: []；本批候选均有明确分区。
- 禁用与加载点击（implemented）：UiButton.Root 将 loading/disabled 设为禁用且 click guard 拦截；装载开始前缓存几何尺寸。 证据：[src/ui/UiButton.vue:44](../../src/ui/UiButton.vue#L44)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBtn/VBtn.js:176](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBtn/VBtn.js#L176)。
- 路由对象与当前路由（partial）：静态代码显示对象 to 会生成无 href 的 anchor；本地没有读取 RouterLink/useLink 或当前路由，也没有 replace/exact/active 路由整合。未在本轮安装路由器实测。 证据：[src/ui/UiButton.vue:64](../../src/ui/UiButton.vue#L64)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBtn/VBtn.js:121](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBtn/VBtn.js#L121)。
- Button group selected event（missing）：VBtn emits group:selected on group selection changes; UiButton has no matching declared emit, so the local group state change does not provide the same public event. 证据：[src/ui/UiButton.vue:14](../../src/ui/UiButton.vue#L14)、[src/ui/UiButton.vue:64](../../src/ui/UiButton.vue#L64)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBtn/VBtn.js:82](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBtn/VBtn.js#L82)。

- 真实用例待补：路由对象 to、replace/exact/active 路由行为及 prepend/append 槽没有示例。。

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。model string|number|null defaults to empty; number mode parses to number/null. Counter is text.length; upstream counterValue accepts number/function and persistentCounter keeps it shown. (local UiInput:12-37; upstream VTextField.d.ts:66,70; .js:70-71,140)

上游：[VTextField 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VTextField/VTextField.tsx)。

### UTextarea

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。model string defaults empty, rows defaults 5, autoGrow caps by maxRows; no pixel maxHeight prop. (local UiTextarea:7-16,28-62; upstream VTextarea.d.ts:75-76)

缺失插槽：`counter`、`details`、`prepend`、`append`、`message`、`label`、`clear`、`prepend-inner`、`append-inner`、`loader`、`default`。

### USwitch

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。model defaults false, maps value/trueValue/falseValue and respects readonly/disabled. (local UiSwitch:9-24) Missing VSwitch thumb/track slots and update:focused/update:indeterminate contracts.

上游：[VSwitch 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VSwitch/VSwitch.tsx)。

缺失事件：`update:focused`、`update:indeterminate`。

缺失插槽：`prepend`、`append`、`details`、`message`、`label`、`input`、`loader`、`thumb`、`track-true`、`track-false`、`default`。

上游：[VTooltip 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VTooltip/VTooltip.tsx)。

### UChip

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。本地 selected 可由选择组控制，closable 关闭发 MouseEvent；disabled 阻止选择；tone/variant 是本库外观协议。

上游：[VChip 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VChip/VChip.tsx)。

缺失属性：`modelValue`、`border`、`elevation`、`hoverElevation`、`rounded`、`tile`、`size`、`tag`、`selectedClass`、`href`、`replace`、`to`、`exact`、`activeClass`、`appendAvatar`、`appendIcon`、`baseColor`、`closeIcon`、`draggable`、`filter`、`filterIcon`、`label`、`link`、`pill`、`prependAvatar`。

缺失事件：`update:modelValue`、`click:close`、`group:selected`。

缺失插槽：`label`、`prepend`、`append`、`close`、`filter`。

- 契约：本地 selected 可由选择组控制，closable 关闭发 MouseEvent；disabled 阻止选择；tone/variant 是本库外观协议。
- 契约：官方 VChip 使用 modelValue 受控 active 状态并发 update:modelValue；本地 selected 与组件模型不是同名兼容协议。
- 契约：VChip click:close 与 group:selected 不由本地 close 事件替代；本地 close 虽带 MouseEvent 但事件名不同。普通 click/onClick/onClickOnce 由 UChip.vue:30 明确转给 UiBadge 根节点处理；default slot 名存在但缺少 VChip 的 selection scope。
- 属性候选逐项判定：31项；确认缺失25、实现/转发2、保留约定4、待验证0。
- 已有实现/转发属性：`onClick`、`onClickOnce`。
- 外观/本库约定差异属性：`theme`、`density`、`prependIcon`、`text`。
- 属性依据：候选 31 项均已逐项人工对照上游发布类型/runtime 与本地声明/模板；上游候选定义见 artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.d.ts:21, - 属性依据：theme 按项目自有 tokens/theme context 约定列为 intentionallyDifferent，不表示接受 Vuetify theme 配置；其余颜色/尺寸/媒体属性仍按实际实现单项判断。
- 属性依据：unverified: []；本批候选均有明确分区。
- 关闭事件 payload（different）：本地 close 与官方 click:close 都携带 MouseEvent，但事件名不同，不能作为同一监听器协议。 证据：[src/ui/UChip.vue:21](../../src/ui/UChip.vue#L21)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js:30](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js#L30)。
- 受控激活模型（partial）：本地只接受 selected/group 选择态，没有 VChip modelValue 与 update:modelValue 受控契约。 证据：[src/ui/UChip.vue:9](../../src/ui/UChip.vue#L9)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js:66](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js#L66)。
- Click listener properties（implemented）：VChip declares onClick/onClickOnce listeners; UChip explicitly forwards $attrs to UiBadge, whose single root span receives those DOM click listeners. 证据：[src/ui/UChip.vue:30](../../src/ui/UChip.vue#L30)、[src/ui/UiBadge.vue:22](../../src/ui/UiBadge.vue#L22)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js:70](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js#L70)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js:196](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js#L196)。
- Close event name and payload（different）：Both deliver a MouseEvent, but VChip emits click:close and UChip emits close; the payload match does not make the event names compatible. 证据：[src/ui/UChip.vue:21](../../src/ui/UChip.vue#L21)、[src/ui/UChip.vue:30](../../src/ui/UChip.vue#L30)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js:95](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js#L95)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js:140](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js#L140)。
- Group selected event（missing）：VChip emits group:selected; UChip only declares close and calls the local selection group toggle without emitting the Vuetify event. 证据：[src/ui/UChip.vue:21](../../src/ui/UChip.vue#L21)、[src/ui/UChip.vue:33](../../src/ui/UChip.vue#L33)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js:97](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.js#L97)。
- Default slot selection scope（partial）：Both have a default slot, but VChip supplies isSelected/selectedClass/select/toggle/value/disabled; UChip renders its default slot without scope values. 证据：[src/ui/UChip.vue:33](../../src/ui/UChip.vue#L33)、[src/ui/UChip.vue:34](../../src/ui/UChip.vue#L34)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.d.ts:6](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VChip/VChip.d.ts#L6)。
- 真实用例已覆盖：FeedbackDemo 展示 tone/variant；chip SFC 展示 closable 和恢复。。

### UAlert

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。本地 tone 默认 info、dense=false；error 用 alert，其它 tone 用 status；只公开 icon/default/actions 槽。

### UAutocomplete

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。model/search are separate and search defaults empty; items normalize/filter with multiple/create paths. (src/ui/UAutocomplete.vue:14-53) Candidate audit confirms menu/list/filter and auto-selection options are not implemented.

上游：[VAutocomplete 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VAutocomplete/VAutocomplete.tsx)。

### UBottomNavigation

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。VBottomNavigation exposes active state/event plus item group; local modelValue provides one selection to its slot but no active/group contract. Upstream name keys its layout item; local uses a fixed layout key and tag is fixed.

上游：[VBottomNavigation 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VBottomNavigation/VBottomNavigation.tsx)。

缺失属性：`name`、`order`、`tag`、`multiple`、`mandatory`、`max`、`disabled`、`active`。

缺失事件：`update:active`。

- 契约：Local declaration: props=['modelValue', 'height', 'fixed', 'absolute']; emits=['update:modelValue']; slots=['default']. Local defaults/model: height=56,fixed=false,absolute=false; model uncontrolled when absent.
- 契约：VBottomNavigation exposes active state/event plus item group; local modelValue provides one selection to its slot but no active/group contract. Upstream name keys its layout item; local uses a fixed layout key and tag is fixed.
- 契约：Official runtime and published types were checked for stable prop/default/model/event/slot contracts; internal symbols and event-listener extraction keys were excluded.
- 属性候选逐项判定：21项；确认缺失8、实现/转发0、保留约定13、待验证0。
- 外观/本库约定差异属性：`theme`、`border`、`density`、`elevation`、`hoverElevation`、`rounded`、`tile`、`selectedClass`、`baseColor`、`bgColor`、`color`、`grow`、`mode`。
- 属性依据：missing candidates ['name', 'order', 'tag', 'multiple', 'mandatory', 'max', 'disabled', 'active']: absent from local declared prop surface (src/ui/UBottomNavigation.vue:6); upstream published type evidence ['artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBottomNavigation/VBottomNavigation.d.ts:7', 'artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBottomNavigation/VBottomNavigation.d.ts:8', 'artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBottomNavigation/VBottomNavigation.d.ts:16'].
- 属性依据：intentionallyDifferent styling/geometry candidates ['theme', 'border', 'density', 'elevation', 'hoverElevation', 'rounded', 'tile', 'selectedClass', 'baseColor', 'bgColor', 'color', 'grow', 'mode']: the local component retains its own class/style contract and has no prop binding for these upstream presentation controls (src/ui/UBottomNavigation.vue:6); upstream type evidence ['artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBottomNavigation/VBottomNavigation.d.ts:4', 'artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBottomNavigation/VBottomNavigation.d.ts:10', 'artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VBottomNavigation/VBottomNavigation.d.ts:11']. 存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。Local month/week/day calendar with date-string model and date-only event cards. Manual review found missing category/interval/event configuration, upstream event listeners and named slots; same-named event/day slot payloads differ. (src/ui/UCalendar.vue:8-34)

上游：[VCalendar 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VCalendar/VCalendar.tsx)。

缺失插槽：`category`、`day-body`、`day-header`、`day-label`、`day-label-header`、`day-month`、`interval`、`interval-header`、`days`。

### UCarousel

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。Both VCarousel and local interval defaults are 6000ms; local timer clamps configured intervals to at least 1000ms while upstream accepts any positive interval. Reverse/transition/delimiter/progress/icon candidates are visual-only. Default slot scope is custom rather than upstream group.

上游：[VCarousel 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VCarousel/VCarousel.tsx)。

缺失属性：`tag`、`continuous`、`showArrows`、`direction`、`mandatory`。

缺失插槽：`item`、`prev`、`next`。

- 契约：Local declaration: props=['interval', 'cycle', 'disabled', 'label', 'touch', 'keyboard', 'ripple', 'modelValue']; emits=['update:modelValue']; slots=['default']. Local defaults/model: interval=6000, cycle=true, touch=true, keyboard=true, ripple=true; model=null.
- 契约：Both VCarousel and local interval defaults are 6000ms; local timer clamps configured intervals to at least 1000ms while upstream accepts any positive interval. Reverse/transition/delimiter/progress/icon candidates are visual-only. Default slot scope is custom rather than upstream group.
- 契约：Official runtime and published types were checked for stable prop/default/model/event/slot contracts; internal symbols and event-listener extraction keys were excluded.
- 属性候选逐项判定：20项；确认缺失5、实现/转发0、保留约定15、待验证0。
- 外观/本库约定差异属性：`theme`、`nextIcon`、`prevIcon`、`reverse`、`verticalArrows`、`selectedClass`、`crossfade`、`transitionDuration`、`color`、`delimiterIcon`、`height`、`hideDelimiters`、`hideDelimiterBackground`、`progress`、`verticalDelimiters`。

### UCarouselItem

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。VCarouselItem composes VImg with VWindowItem; local wrapper composes only UWindowItem, so image fetching/loading/alternative text and disabled behavior are absent. The upstream wrapper declares no emits and does not forward VWindowItem group:selected; that event is not its contract. VImg slots have no scope.

上游：[VCarouselItem 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VCarousel/VCarouselItem.tsx)。

缺失属性：`disabled`、`alt`、`draggable`、`lazySrc`、`options`、`sizes`、`src`、`crossorigin`、`referrerpolicy`、`srcset`。

### UColorPicker

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。model defaults #000000; HSV sliders plus optional hex/swatches are available; input mode is fixed. (src/ui/UColorPicker.vue:13-26,33-35; VColorPicker.d.ts:39-40)

缺失事件：`update:mode`。

### UCombobox

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。Local wraps UAutocomplete, forwards model/search and every named slot, and enables create behavior. (src/ui/UCombobox.vue:8-25)

上游：[VCombobox 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VCombobox/VCombobox.tsx)。

缺失属性：`focused`、`customFilter`、`customKeyFilter`、`filterKeys`、`filterMode`、`ignoreAccents`、`noFilter`、`messages`、`itemChildren`、`itemType`、`counterValue`、`persistentCounter`、`closeText`、`openText`、`closableChips`、`eager`、`hideNoData`、`listProps`、`menu`、`menuElevation`、`menuIcon`、`menuProps`、`noAutoScroll`、`alwaysFilter`、`autoSelectFirst`、`clearOnSelect`、`delimiters`、`closeOnInputClick`、`trimValues`。

### UDataIterator

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。本地 items 必填，itemsPerPage 默认 10，只受控 page/sortBy；default scope 为 raw visible/all items、page/pageCount、sortBy、nextPage/prevPage、total。

上游：[VDataIterator 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VDataIterator/VDataIterator.tsx)。

缺失属性：`itemsLength`、`loading`、`modelValue`、`itemSelectable`、`showSelect`、`selectStrategy`、`expanded`、`groupBy`、`opened`、`showExpand`、`customKeyFilter`、`filterKeys`、`filterMode`、`ignoreAccents`、`noFilter`、`expandOnClick`、`expandStrategy`、`initialSortOrder`、`customKeySort`、`multiSort`、`mustSort`、`openAll`、`groupKey`、`valueComparator`、`tag`、`transition`、`pageBy`、`itemValue`、`returnObject`。

缺失事件：`update:modelValue`、`update:itemsPerPage`、`update:groupBy`、`update:expanded`、`update:opened`、`update:options`、`update:currentItems`。

缺失插槽：`header`、`footer`、`loader`、`no-data`。

- 契约：本地 items 必填，itemsPerPage 默认 10，只受控 page/sortBy；default scope 为 raw visible/all items、page/pageCount、sortBy、nextPage/prevPage、total。
- 契约：官方 VDataIterator 默认 itemsPerPage=5，提供 selection/expand/group 模型、loading/no-data/loading 状态和 header/footer/loader/no-data slots；本地是轻量分页筛选迭代器，不具备这组协议。
- 契约：官方发 update:options、update:currentItems 等模型事件；本地只发 page/sortBy updates；selection/group/expand 候选和 header/footer/loader/no-data 槽均逐项核对。
- 属性候选逐项判定：29项；确认缺失29、实现/转发0、保留约定0、待验证0。

### UDataTableVirtual

### UDateInput

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。Local VDateInput wraps a custom date picker with string model and firstDayOfWeek only. firstDayOfYear/showWeek are not carried into the picker; firstDayOfYear also affects upstream week numbering.

上游：[VDateInput 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VDateInput/VDateInput.tsx)。

缺失属性：`focused`、`validationValue`、`messages`、`counterValue`、`persistentCounter`、`noMonthPicker`、`viewMode`、`month`、`showAdjacentMonths`、`weekdays`、`weeksInMonth`、`firstDayOfWeek`、`weekdayFormat`、`hideWeekdays`、`multiple`、`showWeek`、`events`、`eventColor`、`noAutoNavigation`、`previewValue`、`year`、`allowedMonths`、`allowedYears`、`header`、`headerColor`、`headerDateFormat`、`landscapeHeaderWidth`、`inputFormat`、`displayFormat`、`menu`、`menuProps`、`openOnFocus`、`updateOn`、`pickerProps`、`firstDayOfYear`。

缺失事件：`update:focused`、`update:menu`、`save`、`cancel`。

缺失插槽：`prepend`、`append`、`append-inner`、`clear`、`counter`、`details`、`label`、`loader`、`message`、`prepend-inner`、`title`、`header`、`day`、`month`、`year`、`actions`。

- 契约：Local model is ISO string|string[]|null, default null; typed text accepts ISO dates/range and validates min/max/allowedDates. (src/ui/UDateInput.vue:14-38)

### UExpansionPanels

### UHotkey

同名职责不同；源码对照。确认同名职责不同：本地是键盘监听，上游是跨平台快捷键显示；后续兼容策略另定。

上游：[VHotkey 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VHotkey/VHotkey.tsx)。

- 契约：已确认同名职责不同：本地 UHotkey 是窗口级键盘输入监听器，Vuetify VHotkey 是跨平台快捷键显示组件；审计判断已完成，后续兼容策略另定。
- 契约：本地 keys 必填，disabled=false、preventDefault=true、allowInput=false；trigger 发 KeyboardEvent；挂载注册 window keydown，卸载移除。不要因未发 VHotkey 视觉标记而否定本地触发功能。
- 属性候选逐项判定：14项；确认缺失0、实现/转发0、保留约定14、待验证0。
- 外观/本库约定差异属性：`theme`、`border`、`elevation`、`hoverElevation`、`rounded`、`tile`、`displayMode`、`keyMap`、`platform`、`inline`、`prefix`、`suffix`、`variant`、`color`。

### UInfiniteScroll

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。本地与官方都提供无限加载；本地 direction 表示 start/end sentinel 侧，上游 direction 表示 vertical/horizontal，并另有 side/mode/load payload；这些协议存在明确差异。

上游：[VInfiniteScroll 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VInfiniteScroll/VInfiniteScroll.tsx)。

缺失属性：`side`、`mode`、`tag`、`height`、`maxHeight`、`maxWidth`、`minHeight`、`minWidth`、`width`、`color`。

- 契约：本地 load payload 为 {done(status?)}；官方为 {side,done(status)}；本地另提供 retry/reset/loading/empty/error/load-more slot scopes。
- 契约：本地与官方都提供无限加载，因此归为 partial；本地 direction=start|end 表示 sentinel 侧，上游 direction=vertical|horizontal、side=start|end|both、mode=intersect|manual，load payload 也分别为 {done(status?)} 与 {side,done(status)}。
- 属性候选逐项判定：13项；确认缺失10、实现/转发0、保留约定3、待验证0。
- 外观/本库约定差异属性：`margin`、`loadMoreText`、`emptyText`。

- 属性依据：missing 分组：tag, height, maxHeight, maxWidth, minHeight, minWidth, width, color, side, mode。这些候选未见本地组件行为实现，按属性协议缺口记录。
- 属性依据：intentionallyDifferent 分组：margin, loadMoreText, emptyText。同为无限加载但协议不同；margin 对应本地 rootMargin，loadMoreText/emptyText 由 load-more/empty 插槽表达；side/mode、轴向 direction 与 load payload 仍是明确 API 差异。
- 属性依据：映射已按 Root 指导列为 partial；本地 direction=start|end 表示 sentinel 侧，上游 direction=vertical|horizontal、side=start|end|both、mode=intersect|manual；本地 load payload {done(status?)} 与上游 {side, done(status)} 不同。

- 可见 sentinel、disabled 和 done 状态（implemented）：本地 IntersectionObserver 只对当前 start/end 哨兵触发；busy/done/disabled 阻止重入，done(empty/error) 切换状态，retry 重试。 证据：[src/ui/UInfiniteScroll.vue:15](../../src/ui/UInfiniteScroll.vue#L15)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VInfiniteScroll/VInfiniteScroll.js:141](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VInfiniteScroll/VInfiniteScroll.js#L141)。
- 轴向 direction、side/mode 与 load payload（different）：本地 direction=start|end 决定首尾 sentinel，官方 direction=vertical|horizontal 决定滚动轴，side 决定加载侧，mode 支持 manual；官方 load 还带 side。 证据：[src/ui/UInfiniteScroll.vue:5](../../src/ui/UInfiniteScroll.vue#L5)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VInfiniteScroll/VInfiniteScroll.js:16](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VInfiniteScroll/VInfiniteScroll.js#L16)。
- 真实用例已覆盖：Infinite scroll SFC 连续加载并通过 done(empty) 终止。。
- 用例源码：[src/ui/docs/LiveExample.vue](../../src/ui/docs/LiveExample.vue)、[src/ui/docs/component-examples/infinite-scroll.vue](../../src/ui/docs/component-examples/infinite-scroll.vue)。
- 真实用例待补：真实 SFC 尚无 horizontal、both-side、manual mode、error/retry/reset 或 side payload 场景。。

Local parses Number(text) and always renders a text input; precision only rounds stepping and there is no display-format state. 证据：[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:12](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L12)、[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:14-18](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L14)、[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:20-22](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L20)、[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:48-55](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L48)、[src/ui/specialized-inputs.ts:6-18](../../src/ui/specialized-inputs.ts#L6)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js:20-64](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js#L20)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js:90-115](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js#L90)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js:135-151](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js#L135)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/format.js:1-40](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/format.js#L1)。
- inherited focused, validation and counter behavior（partial）：The local component uses its model as the validation value and exposes FormControl errors, but has no focused model/update event, explicit error flag, arbitrary messages, counterValue/persistentCounter rendering or clearable/loading/prefix/suffix behavior. Its FormControl counter prop is declared but not rendered by UiControlFrame. 证据：[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:12-18](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L12)、[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:33-38](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L33)、[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:46-55](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L46)、[src/ui/form.ts:7-30](../../src/ui/form.ts#L7)、[src/ui/UiControlFrame.vue:7-20](../../src/ui/UiControlFrame.vue#L7)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js:70-73](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js#L70)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js:408-430](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js#L408)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VTextField/VTextField.d.ts:392-420](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VTextField/VTextField.d.ts#L392)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VInput/VInput.d.ts:24-57](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VInput/VInput.d.ts#L24)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VInput/VInput.d.ts:78-100](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VInput/VInput.d.ts#L78)。
- long press repeat（missing）：The upstream controls begin a hold timer on pointerdown, repeat stepping on an interval, and stop on pointerup/cancel. Local controls step once on click and do not install pointer hold handlers. 证据：[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:34-38](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L34)、[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:48-55](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L48)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js:269-287](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js#L269)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js:324-368](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js#L324)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/hold.js:12-28](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/hold.js#L12)。
- minimum and maximum defaults（different）：Local min/max default to undefined and precision has no explicit default; upstream min/max default to Number.MIN_SAFE_INTEGER/Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER and precision defaults to 0. Step defaults to 1 in both. 证据：[src/ui/UNumberInput.vue:12](../../src/ui/UNumberInput.vue#L12)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js:31-46](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VNumberInput/VNumberInput.js#L31)。

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。click:outside exists but emits no event/target payload; activator/default scopes are {props,activatorProps,open}/{close}, unlike upstream. Visual opacity/z-index/transition candidates excluded.

### UParallax

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。本地 speed=0.3，disabled 可选；speed clamp 到 ±1，按最近滚动祖先与 reduced motion 计算 offset/ratio；background 与 default({offset,ratio}) 是本库扩展。

上游：[VParallax 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VParallax/VParallax.tsx)。

缺失属性：`scale`、`src`、`srcset`、`lazySrc`。

缺失事件：`loadstart`、`load`、`error`。

缺失插槽：`placeholder`、`error`、`sources`。

- 契约：本地 speed=0.3，disabled 可选；speed clamp 到 ±1，按最近滚动祖先与 reduced motion 计算 offset/ratio；background 与 default({offset,ratio}) 是本库扩展。
- 契约：官方 scale 默认 0.5；VParallax 实际根是 VImg 并把 slots/attrs 传入，因此 src 等图像配置、VImg load/error/loadstart 与 placeholder/error/sources 都可用。本地根为 div，src attr 不会创建图片。
- 属性候选逐项判定：1项；确认缺失1、实现/转发0、保留约定0、待验证0。
- 属性依据：候选 1 项均已逐项人工对照上游发布类型/runtime 与本地声明/模板；上游候选定义见 artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VParallax/VParallax.d.ts:5，runtime 入口见 artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VParallax/VParallax.js:16，本地 props/render 见 src/ui/UParallax.vue:78 / src/ui/UParallax.vue:78。missing 数组中的稳定候选在本地未声明或无等价 helper/child 协议；不把普通 DOM attrs fallthrough 计作功能支持。
- 属性依据：missing 分组：scale。这些候选未见本地组件行为实现，按属性协议缺口记录。
- 属性依据：supportedViaForwarding: []；没有候选仅因通用 v-bind="$attrs"、原生属性或 API 名称相似而被认定完整支持。
- 属性依据：unverified: []；本批候选均有明确分区。
- 嵌套滚动/减动效与 speed 扩展（implemented）：本地在捕获阶段接收 UScrollArea/native overflow 滚动，计算可视容器 bounds；disabled/reduced motion 将 offset 归零并清理监听。 证据：[src/ui/UParallax.vue:25](../../src/ui/UParallax.vue#L25)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VParallax/VParallax.js:35](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VParallax/VParallax.js#L35)。
- VImg 图像协议与 scale 参数（partial）：本地没有 scale；根 div 无 VImg 图片加载路径。官方运行时将全部 slots 传给 VImg，fallthrough src/srcset 与 image slots/event 可以工作；本地 speed 不能代替 scale。 证据：[src/ui/UParallax.vue:79](../../src/ui/UParallax.vue#L79)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VParallax/VParallax.js:82](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VParallax/VParallax.js#L82)。

### UPullToRefresh

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。本地 threshold 默认72、disabled=false；只处理触摸向下且容器 scrollTop=0；refresh 发 {done()}；indicator scope distance/refreshing。

上游：[VPullToRefresh 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VPullToRefresh/VPullToRefresh.tsx)。

缺失属性：`pullDownThreshold`。

缺失事件：`load`。

缺失插槽：`pullDownPanel`。

- 契约：本地 threshold 默认72、disabled=false；只处理触摸向下且容器 scrollTop=0；refresh 发 {done()}；indicator scope distance/refreshing。
- 契约：官方 pullDownThreshold 默认64；load 发 {done()}，并兼容 mouse down/up；pullDownPanel scope 为 canRefresh/goingUp/refreshing。
- 契约：VPullToRefresh 公共 runtime/type 声明 pullDownThreshold=64；本地使用 threshold=72，功能相近但并未读取/支持 pullDownThreshold 这个稳定 prop 名，故列为手工确认的属性缺口。自动候选 null 不等于零缺口。
- 属性候选逐项判定：0项；确认缺失0、实现/转发0、保留约定0、待验证0。
- 属性依据：batch 自动候选为 null；已人工检查上游公开 runtime/types artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VPullToRefresh/VPullToRefresh.d.ts:13-14 / artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VPullToRefresh/VPullToRefresh.js:13-14（disabled、pullDownThreshold=64），本地 src/ui/UPullToRefresh.vue:5 使用 threshold=72。虽然阈值功能相近，标准 pullDownThreshold 名称没有实现，已另外列入 confirmedMissingProps；null 不等于零缺口。
- 属性依据：supportedViaForwarding: []；没有候选仅因通用 v-bind="$attrs"、原生属性或 API 名称相似而被认定完整支持。

### USnackbar

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。共享 SnackbarProps：timeout=5000、location=bottom center、variant=elevated、closeText=关闭、rounded=true；model default=false；本地额外 timeout、after-enter/after-leave 事件。

上游：[VSnackbar 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VSnackbar/VSnackbar.tsx)。

缺失属性：`closeOnBack`、`openOnHover`、`locationStrategy`、`origin`、`height`、`maxHeight`、`maxWidth`、`minHeight`、`minWidth`、`width`、`position`、`transition`、`closeDelay`、`openDelay`、`target`、`openOnClick`、`openOnFocus`、`closeOnContentClick`、`eager`、`absolute`、`contentClass`、`contentProps`、`disabled`、`opacity`、`zIndex`、`collapsed`、`queueGap`、`prependAvatar`。

缺失事件：`click:outside`。

- 契约：共享 SnackbarProps：timeout=5000、location=bottom center、variant=elevated、closeText=关闭、rounded=true；model default=false；本地额外 timeout、after-enter/after-leave 事件。
- 契约：本地支持 activator/default/prepend/header/title/text/actions 槽，pause on pointer/focus/document hidden；官方继承 VOverlay 的关闭/定位配置和 VSnackbar 事件。
- 契约：VSnackbar runtime 从 VOverlay propsFactory 中 omit scrim，并在内部固定 scrim=false，因此 scrim 不是 VSnackbar 公共缺失 prop；类型继承的 click:outside 未在本地发出。

### USparkline

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。本地 values 必填，width=120/height=40；min/max 可选；slot scope points/path，默认画单条直线。

### UStepper

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。Root model is controlled; item disabled is watched into a local blocked set, so movement skips blocked steps. go() reads current parent disabled for exposed movement and item clicks; createGroup snapshots mandatory, which UStepperItem.canActivate reads. Parent editable/mobile override/breakpoint/tag and the public location anchor are absent.

上游：[VStepper 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VStepper/VStepper.tsx)。

缺失属性：`mobile`、`mobileBreakpoint`、`tag`、`multiple`、`max`、`location`、`prevText`、`nextText`、`editable`、`hideActions`、`items`、`itemTitle`、`itemValue`、`itemProps`、`nonLinear`。

缺失插槽：`actions`、`header`、`header-item`、`icon`、`title`、`subtitle`、`item`、`prev`、`next`。

- 契约：Local declaration: props=['disabled', 'mandatory', 'linear', 'modelValue']; emits=['update:modelValue']; slots=['default']. Local defaults/model: model=null, mandatory=true, linear=false, disabled=false.
- 契约：Root model is controlled; item disabled is watched into a local blocked set, so movement skips blocked steps. go() reads current parent disabled for exposed movement and item clicks; createGroup snapshots mandatory, which UStepperItem.canActivate reads. Parent editable/mobile override/breakpoint/tag and the public location anchor are absent.
- 契约：Official runtime and published types were checked for stable prop/default/model/event/slot contracts; internal symbols and event-listener extraction keys were excluded.
- 属性候选逐项判定：36项；确认缺失15、实现/转发0、保留约定21、待验证0。
- 外观/本库约定差异属性：`theme`、`border`、`elevation`、`hoverElevation`、`rounded`、`tile`、`selectedClass`、`height`、`maxHeight`、`maxWidth`、`minHeight`、`minWidth`、`width`、`position`、`color`、`altLabels`、`bgColor`、`completeIcon`、`editIcon`、`errorIcon`、`flat`。
- 属性依据：missing candidates ['mobile', 'mobileBreakpoint', 'tag', 'multiple', 'max', 'location', 'prevText', 'nextText', 'editable', 'hideActions', 'items', 'itemTitle', 'itemValue', 'itemProps', 'nonLinear']: absent from local declared prop surface (src/ui/UStepper.vue:6); upstream published type evidence ['artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.d.ts:26', 'artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.d.ts:27', 'artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.d.ts:144'].
- 属性依据：intentionallyDifferent styling/geometry candidates ['theme', 'border', 'elevation', 'hoverElevation', 'rounded', 'tile', 'selectedClass', 'height', 'maxHeight', 'maxWidth', 'minHeight', 'minWidth', 'width', 'position', 'color', 'altLabels', 'bgColor', 'completeIcon', 'editIcon', 'errorIcon', 'flat']: the local component retains its own class/style contract and has no prop binding for these upstream presentation controls (src/ui/UStepper.vue:6); upstream type evidence ['artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.d.ts:134', 'artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.d.ts:139', 'artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.d.ts:140']. This is an intentional visual/API difference, not claimed support; arbitrary attrs are not credited.
- 属性依据：location is a public VSheet anchor prop inherited by VStepper (artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.js:48-56; artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.d.ts:322-324). Local src/ui/UStepper.vue declares only disabled/mandatory/linear and renders a fixed div (src/ui/UStepper.vue:6,32), so location is missing rather than implicitly forwarded.
- linear movement and disabled items（implemented）：move checks the child blocked Set then calls go, which checks current parent disabled and linear progression; UStepperItem clicks call go directly. 证据：[src/ui/UStepper.vue:11-24](../../src/ui/UStepper.vue#L11)、[src/ui/UStepperItem.vue:10-18](../../src/ui/UStepperItem.vue#L10)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/composables/group.js:93-126](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/composables/group.js#L93)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/composables/group.js:168-184](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/composables/group.js#L168)。
- dynamic parent disabled/mandatory（partial）：Parent disabled is read live by go; mandatory is captured by createGroup at setup and read by child canActivate, so dynamic mandatory state is stale. 证据：[src/ui/UStepper.vue:9-10](../../src/ui/UStepper.vue#L9)、[src/ui/UStepperItem.vue:15](../../src/ui/UStepperItem.vue#L15)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/composables/group.js:93-126](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/composables/group.js#L93)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/composables/group.js:142-165](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/composables/group.js#L142)。
- location anchor configuration（missing）：Upstream VStepper inherits VSheet location props; the local root is a fixed div and neither declares nor consumes location. 证据：[src/ui/UStepper.vue:6](../../src/ui/UStepper.vue#L6)、[src/ui/UStepper.vue:32](../../src/ui/UStepper.vue#L32)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.js:48-56](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.js#L48)、[artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.d.ts:322-324](../../artifacts/upstream-table-audit/package/lib/components/VStepper/VStepper.d.ts#L322)。

### UTreeview

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。Local nested selection and keyboard behavior exist, but itemsRegistration is an absent lifecycle option. Icon/color/indent controls are presentation APIs with no local binding: the local template fixes its chevron/checkbox and uses a constant 20px depth offset without connector lines. separateRoots only changes upstream connector-line recipes, not selection or registration policy.

上游：[VTreeview 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VTreeview/VTreeview.tsx)。

缺失事件：`update:selected`、`click:open`、`click:select`。

缺失插槽：`append`、`divider`、`footer`、`header`、`item`、`prepend`、`subheader`、`subtitle`、`toggle`。

- 契约：Local nested selection and keyboard behavior exist, but itemsRegistration is an absent lifecycle option. Icon/color/indent controls are presentation APIs with no local binding: the local template fixes its chevron/checkbox and uses a constant 20px depth offset without connector lines. separateRoots only changes upstream connector-line recipes, not selection or registration policy.

### UVirtualScroll

存在差异 / 部分覆盖；源码对照。Local fixed-height slicing and scrollToIndex work. Upstream height/maxHeight/maxWidth/minHeight/minWidth/width are stable sizing APIs; local height is its own single viewport prop and fixed-height CSS behavior, not support for the upstream dimensions. renderless is a separate host/scroll-parent contract.

上游：[VVirtualScroll 4.2.4](https://github.com/vuetifyjs/vuetify/blob/v4.2.4/packages/vuetify/src/components/VVirtualScroll/VVirtualScroll.tsx)。

缺失属性：`renderless`。
